# The 10-year strategy factory (2026-10-31)

SPRINT_PLAN Amendment 9 (pre-registered 2026-11-01, before any factory run). Every number is a **BACKTEST**, 1 contract per signal, desk fills, 2% half-spread (MODELED-SPREAD, $0.005 floor), $0.65 per contract per side.

- **HIST 2016-01-04..2024-01-31: option prices MODELED (BS, calibrated IV).** Stock bars are real; the options are Black-Scholes at one flat IV per day. Labeled MODELED everywhere.
- **DEV 2024-08-01..2026-06-30: REAL Alpaca option prices.** Labeled REAL everywhere.
- 2024 is never one number: Jan 2024 is MODELED, Aug-Dec 2024 is REAL. L1 and L2 were never pulled or run.
- Trade files: HIST `/home/box/sprint/data/sprint/trades_factory_hist_v1_2026-10-31.csv.gz`, DEV `/home/box/sprint/data/sprint/trades_factory_dev_v1_2026-10-31.csv.gz`. Specs: `/home/box/sprint/sprint/specs/factory_hist_v1.json` (sha256 97a63d8026d7), `/home/box/sprint/sprint/specs/factory_dev_v1.json` (sha256 9765725b4f81).
- Run log: 42 of 42 factory runs found; HIST spread MODELED-SPREAD; DEV spread MODELED-SPREAD.
- **Trials:** this amendment adds 357 (42 runs + 252 candidate rules + 63 learner models). Deflated Sharpe (information only) uses N = 3742 (run logs, which hold the factory runs, 3427 + 315).
- Not financial advice: evidence for Nick to decide. Nothing here is promoted; a survivor needs a new versioned paper ledger, Nick's yes and 40 forward paper trades (CLAUDE.md step 5).

## Limits

- HIST option prices are MODELED: one flat IV per day, no smile, no intraday IV change, a price every minute, a 0DTE expiry every session (before Nov 2022 SPY/QQQ/IWM did not list one every weekday). On days that are already moving real 0DTE IV rises and the model's doesn't, which flattered far-OTM put rules before (Amendment 6, run 5).
- Only three of the 21 strategies had their MODELED tapes checked against real prices (run 4). The others are labeled NOT VALIDATED; holdout 2 on real DEV prices is the deciding test for every rule.
- The noise run uses the noise ledger's exit on modeled tapes: its every-minute 20% ceiling acts on modeled bar highs and lows. With both sides in one run, a reversal exits but does not open the other side (one position per symbol).
- failed_break, otm_p_1030 and otm_c_1100 were studied before (Amendment 6, run 5): their verdicts are not new evidence.
- Most DEV numbers of the unfiltered runs were seen in runs 1-3; only the rules are new on DEV.
- 2% half-spread may be optimistic for thinner 2016-2019 0DTE markets; no 5% stress in this run.
